## Document 035: 2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-11-09_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更_65c10ae5.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

中国が政策金利を翌日物に変更 
～銀行金利市場化の長い道のり～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 月岡直樹 
080-1069-6684 naoki.tsukioka@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 人民銀がリバースレポ翌日物の日常オペを開始。短期金利の誘導精度を高める狙い 
中国の中央銀行に当たる中国人民銀行（PBOC）が10月30日、市場に短期流動性を供給する「売り戻
し条件付き債券購入（リバースレポ）」翌日物の公開市場操作（オペ）を11月2日より毎営業日実施す
ると発表した。これまで原則として毎営業日実施していたリバースレポ7日物から切り替える。翌日物
のオペは固定金利、数量入札の方式で行い、その金利を政策金利と位置づける。インターバンク市場
のレポ取引では翌日物が大半を占めていることから、政策金利を7日物から翌日物に変更することで
期間のズレを解消し、短期市場金利の誘導精度を高める狙いがある。新たなオペ初日の金利は1.25％
であった（前営業日のリバースレポ7日物の金利は1.40％）。 
この動きは、PBOCの潘功勝行長（総裁）が10月19日の講演で明らかにしていた金融政策運営の改革
方針を実行に移したものである。潘氏は「銀行間債券担保付レポ翌日物金利（DR001）は事実上、中
央銀行の金融政策が調節する目標金利となっているが、それと同時に、中央銀行の公開市場操作の主
要な種類と主要な政策金利の期限はなお7日で、両者は完全には一致しない」ことを認めた上で、「適
切なタイミングで公開市場の主要な操作種類の期限を翌日物に切り替え、公開市場の翌日物レポ金利
を主要な政策金利とすることを検討している」と予告していた。この切り替えによって、PBOCの金融
政策運営は米連邦準備制度理事会（FRB）や日本銀行のそれに近づくことになる。 
さらに、潘氏は銀行貸出金利の改革を進めていることも明らかにした。最優遇貸出金利（LPR）を
参照して貸出金利を決定するこれまでの運用に加え、DR001などの短期市場金利を直接参照して金利
を決定する方法を試験的に導入したのである。今後、財務力のある企業は商業銀行と協議の上でDR001
もしくはLPRを参照した貸出金利を選択することができ、小規模企業や個人には引き続きLPRを参照
した金利を適用するとしている。経済誌『財新』によれば、2026年7月以降にDR001を参照した銀行貸
出がすでに全国で100件以上実施されているという。 
PBOCが新たな銀行貸出金利の決定メカニズムを模索する背景には、LPRが「最優遇」から乖離し、
貸出金利の実勢を十分に反映できていないことがあるとみられる。これは、2013年7月に下限規制が撤
廃される前の貸出基準金利が基準の70％まで引き下げを許容していた歴史的経緯から、信用力の高い
顧客にLPR以下の金利で貸出を実行する慣行が残っているためでもある。潘氏は改革を進めると同時
に、すでに実務的な役割を終えている貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を正式に廃止するとした。 
2026 年 11 月 9 日

### Page 2

2

■ 貸出基準金利から最優遇貸出金利へ移行。ただ、実勢金利は「最優遇」から乖離 
PBOCは2010年代以降、銀行金利の市場化改革を慎重に推し進めてきた（図表1）。改革以前はPBOC
が貸出基準金利と預金基準金利を直接コントロールし、商業銀行がその上下一定の範囲内で貸出・預
金金利を決定する制度となっていたが、PBOCは2013年7月に貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃し、同年10月
には全国銀行間コール市場センター（全国銀行間同業拆借中心。以下「コール市場センター」）を通
じて貸出基礎金利（旧LPR）の発表を開始した。旧LPRは、主要9行（のち10行）の報告銀行がコール
市場センターに貸出の最優遇金利を報告し、センター側がそのうち最高値と最低値を除いた報告金利
を報告銀行の貸出残高で加重平均して算出するものであった。 
PBOCは銀行貸出金利が市場原理に基づき決定されるメカニズムへの移行を意図していたが、PBOC
が引き続き基準金利を操作して利下げを行い、報告銀行も基準金利を強く意識して最優遇金利を報告
したため、旧LPRは貸出基準金利に張り付いて変動した。PBOCは「銀行が貸出を実行する際、多くが
なお貸出基準金利を参照して金利を決定し、特に一部の銀行が協調行動を通じて貸出基準金利の一定
図表1 銀行金利の市場化改革 
年月 No. 改革内容 
2004/10  銀行貸出金利の上限規制と銀行預金金利の下限規制を撤廃 
2013/7  ① 銀行貸出金利の下限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の下限は貸出基準金利の 70％） 
2013/10 ② 貸出基礎金利（旧 LPR）の発表を開始 
2015/10 ③ 銀行預金金利の上限規制を撤廃（撤廃前の上限は預金基準金利の 150％） 
2019/8 ④ 貸出市場報告金利（新 LPR）の発表を開始。中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）金利を参照金利に採用 
2024/7 [1] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを開始（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の▲0.2％と＋0.5％） 
 ⑤ リバースレポ 7 日物金利を LPR の参照金利に位置づけ 
2026/6 [2] レポおよびリバースレポ翌日物の臨時オペを変更（金利はリバースレポ 7 日物の±0.25％） 
2026/11 ⑥ リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを開始。その金利を政策金利に位置づけ

倍率（例えば0.9倍）を隠れた下限金利として設定しており、市場金利が実体経済に伝達することを妨
げている」と指摘している。 
そこで、PBOCは2019年8月、貸出市場報告金利（新LPR）と呼ばれる新たなメカニズムを導入する。
中長期流動性の供給ツールである中期貸出ファシリティ（MLF）の金利を参照し、銀行がこれに資金
調達コストや市場需給、リスクプレミアムを反映した最優遇金利をコール市場センターに報告する方
法である。報告銀行は中小銀行を追加して18行（現在20行）まで拡大し、算出方法も加重平均から単
純平均に変更したことで、算出結果がウェイトの高い大手行の報告金利に引っ張られる傾向を排し、
指標としての包括性も高まった。この改革により、LPRは政策金利であるMLF1年物の金利と連動する
ようになったが、LPRが「最優遇」金利から乖離した状態は依然として解消されなかった。報告銀行
が旧LPRの時と同様にMLF金利を強く意識して金利を報告したためと考えられる。 
図表3は新LPRと実際の銀行貸出金利との関係を見たものである。LPRを下回る金利での貸出比率が
年々高まっていることが分かる。LPR1年物は2019年8月～2026年10月までの間に計▲1.25％Pt引き下げ
られているが、実勢金利は資金需要の低迷もあってそれ以上に下がっていることを示唆している。 
一方、銀行預金金利は2015年に上限規制を撤廃した後も、銀行間の過度な預金獲得競争を防ぐため
窓口指導や業界の自主規制の形をとった上限管理を維持し、2022年4月になって10年物国債利回りや
LPR1年物を参照して金利を決定するメカニズムを導入した。上限管理が廃止されたわけではないが、
預金金利は近年、市場金利の低下に伴って低下傾向にある。 
■ 市場化改革の成果を銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着させられるかが今後の焦点に 
PBOCは、2019年頃までに政策金利をリバースレポ7日物金利（短期）と MLF（中長期）の 2本立てと
する金融政策運営の枠組みを整え、新LPRの導入でその枠組みを明確化した（月岡（2024）参照）。
2024年7月には、政策金利をリバースレポ7日物に一本化し、LPRの参照金利もMLFからこれに移行さ
せた。中長期金利を市場の決定に委ねる形へと歩を進めたのである。 
PBOCは同時に、誘導目標とする短期金利を一定の範囲内で安定させるため金利コリドー（回廊）の
図表3 新LPRと銀行貸出金利 図表4 金利コリドー

仕組みも整える（図表4）。2024年7月、流動性を吸収するレポと供給するリバースレポの翌日物の臨
時オペを開始し、レポ金利をリバースレポ7日物の▲0.2％Pt、リバースレポ金利を7日物の＋0.5％Ptに
設定することでコリドーの幅を狭めた。さらに、2026年6月には臨時オペの金利をそれぞれ▲0.25％Pt、
＋0.25％Ptに調整し、誘導目標とするDR001が臨時オペ金利の上下限を継続的に逸脱した場合にオペを
実施すると明確化した。リバースレポ翌日物の日常的なオペを実施する準備を整えた形である。 
このように、PBOCは政策金利による短期市場金利の誘導精度を高めるとともに、市場金利の変化を
実際の銀行貸出金利へと円滑に波及させるメカニズムの構築を進めてきた。今回、政策金利をリバー
スレポ翌日物金利に変更し、LPRを介さずにDR001を直接参照する貸出金利の決定方式を導入したこ
とは、一連の市場化改革における重要な節目と位置づけることができる。DR001参照型の金利は指標
金利としてのLPRを代替するものではなく、PBOCは両者を併存させながら金利波及経路を改善するア
プローチを続けるとみられる。今後は、市場化改革の成果を商業銀行の実際の金利設定行動に定着さ
せられるかが焦点となろう。

［参考文献］

は否定～」Mizuho RT EXPRESS（7月4日）

ネス・エクスプレス（10月26日）

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國聯準會如市場預期，維持政策利率不變。決策官員仍對通膨保持擔憂，也承
認美國經濟正以「強勁速度」成長。聯準會主席 Kevin Warsh 在會後談話中，並
未明確排除進一步緊縮政策的可能，激勵市場對 12 月升息的預期升溫。數據方
面，美國第三季 GDP 年化成長率達 3.4%，高於前值的 2.2%，亦優於市場預期
的 3.2%，印證美國經濟動能強勁。此外，9 月 PCE 與核心 PCE 符合預期，但凸
顯通膨壓力持續偏高。在「通膨高企」與「經濟強韌」雙重因素驅動下，市場預
期聯準會將於 12 月升息，為美元指數提供有力支撐。惟近期受 AI 產業股票波
動加劇影響，部分資金轉入美債避險，導致美元指數在高檔面臨調節性賣壓。
⚫ 儘管受能源價格走揚影響，9 月歐元區通膨率升至 3.8%，但在薪資壓力維持溫
和，且歐洲央行尚未觀察到第二輪通膨效應全面擴散的情況下，歐洲央行上週決
議維持政策利率不變。通膨回升令市場預期歐洲央行將在下次會議升息，為歐元

提供部分支撐，但考量歐元區經濟成長表現仍落後美國，在美元基本面強勁的抑
制下，歐元反彈力道相對有限。目前歐元最大的潛在風險在於部分國家的債券市
場壓力尚未消散，市場對歐洲財政風險仍保持高度警戒。
⚫ 日本央行維持政策利率於 1.25%不變，並且首次明確表示已達成其 2%的通膨目
標；過去日本央行雖認為通膨改善，但始終強調尚未穩定達標，如今首次正式承
認達成 2%目標，代表未來升息門檻進一步下降。此外，日本央行將經濟成長預
測維持在 0.7%至 0.8%，並小幅上修 CPI 預測，令市場預期日央 12 月有望再次
升息，日本貨幣政策正常化將有助日圓結束疲軟走勢。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/2/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.390，低點在 32.676，以 32.478 作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 3 分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，市場等待聯準會利率決議，交投偏謹慎觀望，而外
資在前周大幅匯出之後，轉為雙向進出，台幣止跌回穩，最高來到 32.390 的周內高
點；然而周間，美國科技股財報表現失色，台股賣壓沉重，外資賣超台股並再度轉
為大幅匯出，台幣走貶，最低來到 32.676，不過匯價逢高吸引出口商賣壓出籠，加
上央行提供流動性，台幣貶幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.478。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣偏弱震盪。就國際市場來看，聯準會 10 月利率決策維持利率不
變，但有兩名官員主張升息 1 碼，且聯準會表示通膨仍處於高檔，顯示聯準會對通
膨仍保持警戒，市場預期美國利率將維持高檔，台美利差仍維持在相對高位，不利
台幣表現；另一方面，美國科技股財報表現不如預期，引發市場對 AI 投資回報的疑
慮，對台股也帶來壓力。就台灣市場來看，外資持續調節台股並大幅匯出，10 月累
計賣超將近兆元，資金匯出壓力大，不過一方面央行持續進場調節，加上匯價逢高
預計吸引出口商進場拋匯，料將限制台幣貶值速度。綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢
偏弱。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.300-32.800

外人民幣主要於 6.6600 上方震盪；周間聯準會如預期維持利率不變，美元指數自 102
上方回落，加上中國出口結匯需求持續提供支撐，境外人民幣突破 6.6600 整數關口；
週五人行中間價續創近三年九個月新高，最高來到 6.7138，顯示中國人行允許人民
幣溫和升值的走勢，境外人民幣一度升破 6.6500 整數關口，最高來到 6.6487，終場
收在 6.6528，週線升值 162 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6558，週線升值 146 點。

     預估本週人民幣偏升。就中國國內市場來看，10 月官方製造業 PMI 降至 49.3，
重新落入景氣收縮區間，反映中國內需及製造業動能仍有待改善，不過 9 月出口年
增 36.3%，出口商結匯需求持續提供人民幣支撐，加上人行中間價續創近三年九個

美國利率將維持高檔，美中利差維持在相對高位，將限制人民幣升值的速度與幅度；

＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於上周五宣布利
率維持在 1.25%不變，並表示隨著基礎通膨率大致達到 2%，央行必須持續控制通
膨穩定於此目標，且再次強調貨幣政策已進入更為緊縮階段，未來每隔數月即可能
升息。此外，日本央行在季度報告中，亦將截至 2028 年 3 月財年的核心消費者通
膨預估自三個月前預測的 2.4%上調至 2.6%。日本央行偏鷹立場令市場預期日本最
快將於 12 月再次升息，預計將有利日圓表現。二、聯準會宣布維持利率於 3.75%-
4%區間不變，惟因通膨仍處高位，聯準會重申將致力實現 2%的長期通膨區間， 
FedWatch 顯示 12 月升息機率約七成，美元持穩，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 下行空
間。三、就技術面而言，在日線圖中，USD/JPY 各天期均線趨平，近期匯價大致
在 156 至 158 間震盪，匯價暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現
區間震盪的可能性較高。

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美伊衝突持續以及法國政經情勢動盪等情況下，
歐元走跌，最低來到 1.1111；週間，聯準會和歐洲央行相繼公布利率決議均維持不
變，歐洲央行發表言論偏鷹派，歐元止跌回升至 1.1214；臨近週末，歐元區公布經
濟數據好壞參半，歐元自高點回落，終場收在 1.1158，週線下跌 41 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行如市場預期宣布
存款利率維持在 2.5%不變，並警告能源成本持續上漲將對通膨造成上行風險，暗示
可能在 12 月再次升息，升息預期升溫預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、歐元區第三季 GDP
年增 1.3%，高於市場預期的 1.1%，顯示在能源價格持續上漲的不利因素下，經濟仍
展現韌性，預計有利歐元表現。三、聯準會宣布維持利率在 3.75%-4%不變，並重申
致力控制通膨的立場。此外，美國公布第三季 GDP 成長 3.4%，優於市場預期的 3.2%，
9 月核心 PCE 年增率自前月的 3%上升至 3.1%，在美國經濟強勁及通膨仍高的情況
下，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月升息一碼，美元持穩，預計將限縮歐元走升空間。四、
技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 各天期均線向下發散，不過近期匯價在多次向
下測試 1.1120 附近均未有效跌破，顯示下方仍有買盤支撐。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

### Page 1

Better Interest Spread — Group interest spread widened 8bps qoq to 6.95%, 
mainly driven by better China spread. China spread improved 19bps qoq, as loan 
yield increased 8bps qoq and funding cost was 11bps lower qoq. Taiwan loan spread 
narrowed 7bps qoq, primarily due to 6bps qoq higher funding cost amid Fed rate 
hike and tightening domestic liquidity. 
Muted Loan Growth — Group loans grew by 1% qoq/4% yoy, mainly driven by 
ASEAN portfolio growth and RMB appreciation. Taiwan portfolio was flat qoq/-1% 
yoy. China portfolio was +2% qoq/flat yoy in local currency (+3% qoq/+11% yoy in 
NTD). Management suggested that the China business has achieved positive loan 
growth in the past three months and they expected positive momentum ahead. 
ASEAN portfolio was+2% qoq/+10% yoy,  
Broadly Stable China Asset Quality, Higher ASEAN Credit Cost — Group 
delinquency ratio slightly increased 3bps qoq to 5.16%. Taiwan delinquency ratio 
increased 20bps qoq, while NPL formation improved qoq. China delinquency ratio 
was stable qoq, but NPL formation slightly picked up qoq per our estimates. Group 
credit cost was 2.49%, +11bps qoq, mainly due to higher ASEAN credit costs on the 
back of macro uncertainties especially in Cambodia. 
Implications — Despite steady profit growth in 10M26 and stabilizing China asset 
quality, Chailease shares continued to de-rate this year amid concerns over growth. 
Management sounded more upbeat on China loan growth outlook at the results 
briefing, and the impact from auto financing business on Taiwan loan growth should 
gradually fade. FY26E dividend yield of 6% is attractive, in our view. 
Buy

### Page 2

Chailease Holding (5871.TW)
12 November 2026 Citi Research
2
Chailease Holding
Valuation
Our target price for Chailease of NT$130 is based on 1.2x 2026E P/B, using a Gordon Growth model. Our valuation
methodology takes into account a sustainable ROE estimate of 12.1%, a cost-of-equity assumption of 11.1%, and a long-term
growth rate of 6.0%. We use a blended approach to calculate both risk-free rates and long-term growth rates taking into
account local estimates on these parameters in the major markets in which Chailease operates, namely Taiwan, China, and

Thailand, and weighted them by asset mix.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the Chailease stock from reaching our target price are: [1] slower-than-expected asset
growth momentum; [2] asset quality deterioration in different markets; [3] higher operating expenses, which drag bottom-line
growth; [4] earlier-than-expected capital-raising that dilutes ROE and BVPS; and [5] ASEAN expansion that is slow in
contributing to group earnings. Key upsides risks to our target price include: [1] faster-than-expected asset quality recovery as

macro uncertainties ease; [2] higher-than-expected asset growth momentum.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

buy recommendation and a Catalyst Watch/STV Downside call corresponds to a sell recommendation. Any stock not assigned
to a Catalyst Watch Upside, Catalyst Watch Downside, STV Upside, or STV Downside call is considered Catalyst Watch/STV

研 究 報 告                
穩懋(3105.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
2027 光通訊跟衛星本業雙引擎 認列聯茂收益業外動能強勁 
 投資評價與建議 
公司過往Forward PER落在20~30倍，考量光通訊與衛星業務出貨放量，毛利
率迎來結構性跳升，有利估值重估，維持「增加持股」評等。 
 重點評論分析 
3Q26營收符合預期，毛利率因產品組合優化上升至37.4%：3Q26營收58.6億
元，季增11%，營收符合預期主要係因傳統消費性電子旺季，加上航太衛星與
光電產品動能強勁，毛利率37.4%，季增9.2個百分點，主要係因產品組合優化，
航太衛星與光電產品營收占比過半，加上產能利用率上升至75%，業外收入
6.98億元，主因聯茂貢獻業外EPS約1.61元，稅後EPS 4.55元。 
4Q26營運淡季不淡，2027成⻑動能聚焦Optical跟Infrastructure：(1)4Q26
面臨手機傳統淡季與年底例行性歲修，但受惠於PD產能持續開出與其他產品
線營收持平至微幅成⻑，預估4Q26營收將季增1~3%，毛利率37~39%。(2)光
通訊PD於3Q26逐步放量，4Q26將持續成⻑，預估2027年大幅放量，CW Laser
正進行可靠度測試，預估2027年小量貢獻，顯著營收爆發期落在2028年，EML
處於產品開發與測試階段，2028年才有機會產生營收貢獻。(3)航太衛星帶動
Infrastructure成為3Q26季增幅度最高的產品線，衛星佔Infrastructure營收
比重已升至40%，超越傳統基地台。(4)預估聯茂2027年EPS 72.22元，穩懋採
權益法認列持股23%之投資收益約61.7億元，貢獻EPS約14.56元。 
 投資風險 
光通PD跟雷射放量不如預期、衛星發射遞延。

| 營運概況 
3Q26 產品組合優化帶動毛利率與 EPS 創高：3Q26 營收 58.6 億元，季增 11%，營收符合預期主要係因傳統消費
性電子旺季，加上航太衛星與光電產品動能強勁，毛利率 37.4%，季增 9.2 個百分點，優於財測，主要係因產品
組合優化，航太衛星與光電產品營收占比過半，加上產能利用率上升至 75%，營業利益率 24.2%，業外收入 6.98
億元，因持有聯茂 23%股權並採權益法認列投資收益，聯茂貢獻業外 EPS 約 1.61 元，稅後 EPS 4.55 元。 
4Q26 營運淡季不淡，2027 成⻑動能聚焦 Optical 跟 Infrastructure：(1)儘管 4Q26 面臨手機傳統淡季與年底例
行性歲修，但受惠於 PD 產能持續開出與其他產品線營收持平至微幅成⻑，預估 4Q26 營收將季增 1~3%，毛利
率 37~39%，主要受惠產品組合優化跟產能利用率維持 70%以上。(2)中階手機受臨中國 PA 供應鏈本土化及記憶
體缺貨的逆風，但穩懋聚焦中高階手機市場，動能維持穩健，4Q26 Cellular 營收僅呈現小幅下滑，WiFi 產品在
Router 需求回流下，3Q26 雙位數成⻑，4Q26 呈現持平至微幅成⻑。(3)光通訊 PD 於 3Q26 逐步放量，4Q26
將持續成⻑，預估 2027 年產能完全開出並大幅放量，CW Laser 正進行可靠度測試，預估 2027 年小量貢獻，顯
著營收爆發期落在 2028，EML 處於產品開發與測試階段，2028 年才有機會產生營收貢獻。(4)航太衛星帶動
Infrastructure 成為 3Q26 季增幅度最高的產品線，衛星佔 Infrastructure 營收比重已升至 40%，超越傳統基地
台，目前 GaN 月產能是 300 片左右，公司積極擴充 GaN 產能，預計 1Q27 導入 GaN 6 吋製程生產。(5)聯茂
2027 年爆發性成⻑，預估聯茂 2027 年 EPS 72.22 元，穩懋採權益法認列持股 23%之投資收益約 61.7 億元，貢
獻EPS約14.56元。

| 公 司 簡 介 
穩懋(3105)為全球最大的化合物半導體晶圓代工廠，核心業務聚焦於微波通訊與光電元件的砷化鎵(GaAs)及氮化
鎵(GaN)晶圓代工服務，公司總部及研發中心設於台灣桃園龜山(華亞科技園區)，主要六吋晶圓生產基地位於桃
園華亞廠及龜山廠，總產能約 4.3 萬片，3Q26 產能利用率為 75%。3Q26 年營收比重為 Cellular(手機
PA)25~30%、Infrastructure(通訊基礎建設)35~40%、Wi-Fi 15~20%、Optical 10~15%、其他 3%。主要客
戶包含 Broadcom、Lumentum、Murata 等國際大廠，銷售地區為台灣 8%、亞洲 70%、美洲 15%、歐洲
7%。 
穩懋的微波通訊產品處理高頻訊號的放大與切換，主要元件為異質接面雙載子電晶體(HBT)與假晶高電子遷移率
電晶體(pHEMT) ，用於智慧型手機功率放大器(PA)、Wi-Fi 路由器及低軌衛星設備的射頻(RF)訊號，提供低雜

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다.

## Document 265: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발_5484f7f5

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발_5484f7f5.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27 부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC 으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10 일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450 억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23 년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82 만원에서 64 만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC 에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW 까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

## Document 377: National Bank of Hungary review_ The doves aren’t gone yet

Source PDF: all report pdf/National Bank of Hungary review_ The doves aren’t gone yet.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The National Bank of Hungary kept rates unchanged in
November, but delivered a dovish twist. A rate cut was already
on the table, and improving domestic risk sentiment is creating
more room for manoeuvre. Nevertheless, we only see the next
cuts coming in late 2027 as inﬂation pressures rebuild

Peter Virovacz and Zoltán Homolya
The National Bank of Hungary, Budapest
5.50% Key interest rate
(no change)
As expected
ING's policy view: A dovish twist, but no
rush

### Page 2

The National Bank of Hungary kept the base rate unchanged at
5.50% on 17 November, in line with widespread expectations. This
marked the third consecutive meeting with rates being kept on
hold. While the latest communication revealed somewhat more
room for monetary easing, we think the window of opportunity will
remain narrow.
Looking ahead, the exceptionally favourable domestic inﬂation
backdrop is likely to fade relatively quickly. We see inﬂation

accelerating to 3.0% by December 2026, with repricing at the start
of next year temporarily pushing price pressures closer to 3.5% in
the ﬁrst quarter of 2027. Inﬂation could then approach 4% during
the summer, largely due to base eﬀects. In our view, this
increasingly challenging inﬂation proﬁle means that any near-term
opportunity for easing may prove short-lived.
In the meantime, however, we see the latest dovish shift in
communication potentially testing the waters and gauging market

sensitivity to eventual easing, particularly in a global environment
where hawkishness still dominates. The key litmus test will be the
forint: a muted FX reaction to the dovish rhetoric would give the
central bank greater conﬁdence that some easing could be
delivered without jeopardising market stability. This means that a
possible one-and-done December rate cut has become a clear
dovish risk to our baseline.
However, we believe that any near-term window for easing would

be relatively narrow. As 2027 progresses, the main constraint on
monetary policy is likely to shift gradually from external risks
towards the domestic inﬂation outlook. With inﬂation expected to
move closer to 4% during the summer, we see little room for a
sustained easing cycle over the next six to nine months. Any near-
term easing would therefore be more likely to represent an
opportunistic move than the beginning of a conventional cutting
cycle.

A more durable easing window could open later in 2027, once
inﬂation starts to decelerate more convincingly after the summer. If
this coincides with a benign external environment and a further

our Cookie policy.

decline in the risk premium on Hungarian assets, we see room for
one or two rate cuts in the fourth quarter of 2027. Our baseline
therefore sees the base rate reaching 5.00% by end-2027, followed
by a further reduction to 4.50% by end-2028.
Our three takeaways from the presser

### Page 4

Review Policy rate NBH National Bank of Hungary
Monetary policy Hungary
1. A cut was already on the table. The biggest dovish surprise
came during the press conference. The Monetary Council
considered two options: keeping rates unchanged or cutting
them, while the decision itself was not unanimous. This
suggests that the bar for easing may already be lower than
previously thought. The December Inﬂation Report will now be
particularly important, with Governor Mihály Varga explicitly

stating that favourable developments in Hungary's risk
assessment and inﬂation outlook, together with reduced
external uncertainty, could increase its room for manoeuvre.
2. Hungary's risk story has improved. Perhaps the most notable
change in the narrative is the assessment of Hungary's risk
premium. Varga highlighted the favourable market reception of
the government's ﬁscal plans and its commitment to euro
adoption as factors supporting Hungary's risk assessment and

increasing room for monetary policy. The forint has also
remained stable and is stronger than a year ago, helping to
contain inﬂationary pressures. In other words, domestic
ﬁnancial market developments are becoming less of a
constraint on monetary policy than the Monetary Council
previously considered them to be.
3. The external environment still demands caution. The greater
room for manoeuvre does not mean that the NBH is ready to
embark on an easing cycle. Energy price volatility, elevated

global yields and above-target inﬂation in major economies
continue to warrant caution. At home, inﬂation is also expected
to accelerate from its current 2% level due to fuel prices, tax
changes and other policy measures. This leaves the Monetary
Council in a familiar data-dependent position: its room for
manoeuvre has increased, but whether it can actually use that
room will depend on both the external environment and the
December inﬂation outlook.

## Document 381: NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications
By Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist, Westpac New Zealand
13:16 November 04 2026 Share
The Q3 labour market statistics were weaker than expected. We discuss potential
implications for the RBNZ noting that a December hike remains more likely than not at this
point.
Q3 Labour market data: potential RBNZ implications
The Q3 labour market data were weaker than RBNZ and our expectations.

### Page 2

Today's labour market data seem unequivocally weaker than expected by either us or the RBNZ. While
likely not a gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the arguments of the MPC
doves that a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon - perhaps in December or in the New
Year. It’s likely the case that a bit much was priced into December, so the change in market
probabilities in the wake of today’s data makes sense - although my base case remains that a
December rate rise remains more likely than not.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting though was to be careful about pricing in "done
deals" and that the outlook will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside measures of
inflation expectations, the key domestic data to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the output gap) as this will be a key determinant
of the Bank’s assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
It’s useful to remember in the RBNZ’s forecasting framework that GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy (i.e., the output gap). The Q2 GDP data told us that the
output gap probably narrowed a bit through Q2, mainly reflecting historical revisions. In light of today’s
labour market numbers, the forecast for growth in Q3 may have some downside risk - but there are
conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear. However, it’s probably hard to argue that the level of
excess capacity reduced further in Q3 given today’s data. 
In the eyes of dovish MPC members, the rise in the unemployment rate and other measures important
for the RBNZ's labour market dashboard likely push in the direction of having greater comfort with the
medium-term inflation outlook. The vacancy to unemployment ratio increased a bit, labour constraints
eased a bit, and the unemployment rate has increased. Hence from a labour market perspective it
could be argued that policy could have more time all else equal. The outcome of the election, with the
possibility of a new RBNZ mandate, could increase the prominence of these factors - although this
won’t likely be an explicit factor in the RBNZ’s thinking in December even though it may be in the minds
of individual MPC members.
It could be the case that the argument for increases in the OCR track through 2027 could be
undermined with this data. It’s important to note (probably self-evidently) that there are other data that
will also be relevant. Hence the risk of a pause earlier in the New Year has probably increased with this
data, all else equal. I don't think our forecast peak OCR is really at issue here, but the time taken to get
there could be more flexible. Takes along the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" are very premature and
misplaced, in our view.

A December hike remains more likely than not, but there will be a clear debate to be had between the
MPC hawks and doves.
The forecast peak OCR is likely less impacted than the time to reach that peak. Further pauses in the
tightening cycle could come earlier than currently forecast. 
The outlook for the exchange rate is especially clouded by this data should it take a longer time for
NZ rates to move closer to global norms. 
Watch for updated RBNZ commentary on 10 and 19 November. The Governor’s observation that it
would have been useful to update markets ahead of the October meeting could imply a greater
willingness to comment in the coming weeks.

### Page 3

The outlook for the exchange rate is undermined by this data. I feel very happy about our year-end
target of 79c for the NZD/AUD rate with the risk that a recovery in the NZD takes longer than currently
assumed.
Looking ahead, there are some opportunities for the RBNZ to update markets on their views. Top of
mind is Paul Conway's 10 November speech to the UBS Australasia Conference. The Governor is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November. If the RBNZ has something to say, then it might
happen in conjunction with these events (the 10 November speech is titled “How is the NZ economy
tracking”, so it will be difficult for Conway to avoid saying something about how he is interpreting
today’s data). We also note the Governor's comments in the October MPR that it could have been
useful to have been more flexible about updating markets on emerging views of new economic data.
This might increase the likelihood that these events could be used to provide guidance on the RBNZ’s
views of this data (and other emerging events), especially if it feels that the market’s interpretation is at
odds with its own.

Chief Economist

## Document 384: OPECプラスは増産見送りを継続、2027年の生産枠設定が次の焦点 ～足元の供給不足と将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらむ難しい舵取りが続く～

Source PDF: all report pdf/OPECプラスは増産見送りを継続、2027年の生産枠設定が次の焦点 ～足元の供給不足と将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらむ難しい舵取りが続く～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

World Trends

2026 年 11 月 2 日

1/4 
OPEC プラスは増産見送りを継続、2027 年の生産枠設定が次の焦点 
～足元の供給不足と将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらむ難しい舵取りが続く～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ OPEC プラス有志７ヵ国は 11 月 1 日のオンライン会合で、12 月の原油生産目標を据え置き、
３ヵ月連続で増産を見送った。７ヵ国合計の生産目標は日量約 3,101 万バレルである。 
○ 据え置きの背景には、イラン情勢の緊迫化がある。ホルムズ海峡などで原油輸送に支障が生
じており、目標を引き上げても実際の供給増にはつながりにくい。ＩＥＡによれば、９月の
湾岸産油国の原油輸出量は日量 1,390 万バレルと、前月から増えたものの緊迫化前の約４分
の３にとどまる。石油製品やＬＰＧの輸出は戦前の半分以下にとどまるほか、10 月には中東
のエネルギー関連施設や船舶への攻撃も再び強まった。 
○ 供給制約により原油価格は高水準で推移し、運賃や保険料の上昇も調達コストを押し上げて
いる。一方、高値の長期化は世界経済を下押しし、需要減退を招く懸念がある。ＩＥＡは 2026
年の世界石油需要を前年比で日量260 万バレル減と見込んでいる。 
○ 今後の焦点は、11 月 29 日に予定される OPEC プラス閣僚会合での 2027 年の生産枠の協議と
なる。日量約 200 万バレルの協調減産の扱いや、新たな生産基準の設定が論点だが、イラン
情勢が評価作業を複雑にしている。ＩＥＡは 2027 年に供給が日量 770 万バレル増える一方、
需要の回復は同 200 万バレルにとどまると予想しており、供給回復後には需給が緩む可能性
がある。ただし、OPEC とＩＥＡの需要見通しには隔たりがあり、先行きは不透明である。 
○ 日本など輸入国にとっても、原油高の長期化は貿易収支の悪化やインフレ圧力の増幅につな
がる。当面は、OPEC プラスの会合内容、ホルムズ海峡の航行状況、湾岸産油国の生産・輸出
動向、世界の石油需要を注視する必要がある。

【OPECプラス有志７ヵ国は 12 月の生産目標を据え置き、３ヵ月連続で増産を見送る決定】 
OPEC（石油輸出国機構）にロシアなどの主要産油国を加えた OPEC プラスの有志７ヵ国（サウジ
アラビア、ロシア、イラク、クウェート、カザフスタン、アルジェリア、オマーン）は、11 月１日
にオンライン形式で会合を開催し、12 月の原油生産目標を 11 月から据え置くことを決定した。こ
れにより、３ヵ月連続で増産が見送られることとなった。７ヵ国合計の生産目標は日量約3,101 万

### Page 2

2/4 
バレルとなり、９月以来の水準を維持する。今回の声明では、世界の原油市場の動向や先行きを検
討したうえで生産目標の据え置きを決定したと説明するとともに、各国が協調減産の枠組みを順守
する姿勢をあらためて確認した。

【イラン情勢の緊迫化に伴う供給制約を背景に、生産目標と実際の生産量の乖離が続いている】 
OPEC プラス有志国は、2023 年４月に発表し、翌５月から実施した日量 165 万バレルの自主減産に
ついて、段階的な縮小を進めてきた。2026 年９月には、この自主減産の縮小を完了したものの、そ
の後は 10 月、11 月と生産目標を据え置いており、今回も同様の判断を下した。背景には、イラン
情勢の緊迫化を受けて中東地域からの
原油輸送に支障が生じており、各国が生
産目標を引き上げても、実際の供給拡大
につながりにくい状況が続いているこ
とがある（図１）。特に、世界有数の原油
輸送の要衝であるホルムズ海峡では、船
舶の航行を巡る安全上の懸念が残り、原
油輸送に制約が生じている。湾岸産油国
は代替輸送ルートの活用などによって
輸出の回復を図っているものの、多くの
国の原油生産量は依然として生産目標
を下回っている。こうした状況では、生産目標を引き上げても実際の供給拡大につながる効果は限
定的であり、有志国は現行の生産目標を維持することが妥当と判断したと考えられる。 
実際、ＩＥＡ（国際エネルギー機関）が公表した最新の石油市場月報によれば、９月の湾岸産油
国の原油輸出量は日量1,390 万バレルと、前月から同 440 万バレル増加したものの、イラン情勢が
緊迫化する以前の水準の４分の３程度にとどまる。さらに、原油輸出は回復する一方、石油製品や
ＬＰＧ（液化石油ガス）の輸出量は依然として戦前の半分以下にとどまり、供給制約の解消には至
っていない。10 月には中東地域のエネルギー関連施設や船舶を対象とする攻撃が再び強まり、輸送
を巡る不確実性も高まっている。このため、OPEC プラスが生産目標を引き上げても、それが実際の
原油供給量の増加に結び付くとは限らず、足元の供給不足を解消するうえでは、イラン情勢の安定
化と原油輸送の正常化が不可欠といえる。

### Page 3

3/4 
【原油市場では供給制約が価格を下支えする一方、高値の長期化による需要減退も懸念】 
こうした供給制約は、国際原油市況を
押し上げている（図２）。足元の原油価格
は、イラン情勢が緊迫化する以前の水準
を大きく上回るなど、原油市場では供給
不足への警戒が根強く残っている。さら
に、原油価格の高止まりに加えて海上輸
送に伴う運賃や保険料なども上昇してお
り、原油や石油製品の調達コストを押し
上げる要因となっている。こうした状況
の下では、仮に OPEC プラスが増産に踏み
切ったとしても、供給制約が残る限り原
油価格の大幅な下落にはつながりにくいと考えられる。 
一方、原油価格の高止まりが世界経済の下押し圧力となることも懸念される。原油高に伴うエネ
ルギー価格の上昇は、家計の実質購買力を低下させるとともに、企業の生産・輸送コストを押し上
げることで、個人消費や設備投資などの重しとなる。IEA は、最新の石油市場月報において、2026
年の世界の石油需要が前年比で日量 260 万バレル減少するとの見通しを示しており、供給制約に伴
う石油製品の供給不足や価格高騰などが需要を下押しすると見込んでいる。このように需要の減退
が進めば、将来的には原油需給が緩和する可能性もある。そのため、OPEC プラスにとっては、足元
の供給制約への対応だけでなく、原油高が世界経済や石油需要に及ぼす影響にも配慮した政策運営
が求められる。

【今後の焦点は2027 年の生産枠設定、供給回復に伴う需給緩和の可能性も課題】 
先行きについては、OPEC プラスが11 月29 日に予定する次回会合での協議が注目される。今回の
有志７ヵ国による会合とは異なり、次回は OPEC プラス全体の閣僚会合が予定されており、2027 年
の生産枠を巡る協議が主要な焦点になるとみられる。OPEC プラスは、2023 年４月の自主減産につ
いては９月に縮小を完了したものの、別途実施している日量約 200 万バレルの協調減産措置は 2026
年末まで維持している。2027 年以降の政策運営を巡っては、この減産措置の取り扱いが重要な論点
となる。さらに、加盟各国の持続可能な最大生産能力に関する評価を踏まえ、新たな生産基準を設
定する方針であるものの、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う生産設備や生産能力の拡張計画への影響などが
評価作業を複雑化させている。 
その意味では、今後の OPEC プラスの政策運営は一段と難しさを増すと予想される。イラン情勢
の緊迫化が続き、原油輸送の制約が長期化すれば、世界的な供給不足が続くことで原油価格には上
昇圧力が掛かりやすい。一方で、停戦協議の進展などをきっかけに原油輸送が正常化すれば、これ
まで供給が制約されてきた原油が市場に流入することにより、需給環境が一転して緩和する可能性
がある。ＩＥＡは2027 年の世界の石油供給量が前年比で日量770 万バレル増加すると見込む一方、
図 2 原油価格の推移

### Page 4

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
4/4
需要の回復幅は同 200 万バレルにとどまると予想しており、供給回復の進展に伴う需給緩和の可能
性を示唆している。もっとも、OPEC（日量１億 830 万バレル）とＩＥＡ（同１億 440 万バレル）の
需要見通しの間には大きな隔たりがあり、需給の先行きには不確実性が残る。したがって、OPEC プ
ラスにとっては、供給不足が続く現状と、将来的な供給過剰の双方をにらみながら、加盟国間の利
害を調整していく必要がある。
日本をはじめとする原油輸入国にとっても、原油需給を巡る不確実性は景気や物価の先行きを左
右する重要な要因となる。原油価格の高止まりは、輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支に下押し圧力を
もたらすほか、エネルギー価格や物流コストの上昇を通じてインフレ圧力を増幅させる懸念がある。

一方、原油輸送の正常化によって供給量が回復すれば、原油価格の下落を通じてインフレ圧力が和
らぐことも期待される。ただし、当面は中東情勢を巡る不透明感が根強く、原油価格は地政学的な
動向に左右されやすい状況が続くと考えられる。今後は、次回の OPEC プラス閣僚会合での協議内
容に加え、ホルムズ海峡の航行状況、湾岸産油国の実際の生産・輸出動向、世界の石油需要の変化
などを注意深く見極める必要がある。

## Document 390: Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 14
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#wages #inflation #labour
Q3 Wage Price Index: award wage boost
By Ryan Wells
Economist, Westpac
16:06 November 18 2026 Share
The minimum wage and award decision lifted wages growth, but unevenly – enterprise
arrangements responded more than individual arrangements, consistent with a softer
labour market. Q3 2026: 1.1%qtr, 3.5%yr.
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 1.1% in Q3 2026, slightly above Westpac’s forecast and the
market consensus for a 1.0% gain. That increased the annual growth rate to 3.5%yr – stronger than

### Page 2

Wages by Bargaining Stream
the past six months, but a similar pace to 2025.
Today’s data also showcased the impact of the 2026 Fair Work Commission’s Annual Wage Review,
which handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award wages. This lifted wages in
aggregate, but the gains were uneven.
A significant share of the increase was driven by health care and social assistance, reflecting pay
rises for state government workers covered by enterprise agreements.

In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3. Awards and enterprise arrangements accounted for 64%
of the lift, while individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share for Q3 since 2019.
Enterprise arrangements, which are often directly linked to award outcomes, were more responsive,
while individual arrangements were less so compared to previous September quarters. That is
consistent with a softer labour market and recent business survey evidence that suggests the rise in

private sector labour costs following the award decision quickly normalised.
Overall, we see the Q3 result as a step up in the level of wages from the award decision rather than
evidence of building underlying wage pressure but we will be watching whether the strength in
enterprise bargaining persists.

### Page 3

Westpac has long believed focusing on wage inflation by wage bargaining stream can help to
understand how wages respond to the economic cycle. Australia’s labour market is split into three
categories of pay setting methods:
Australian wage adjustments, including the Fair Work Commission’s (FWC) minimum wage and award
rate decisions, tend to occur at the start of the new financial year in July. The FWC handed down a
4.75% increase for 2026, larger than the decisions from 2025 (3.50%) and 2024 (3.75%), but lower

than in 2023 (5.75%).
In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3 2026. Of that increase, 0.50ppts were attributed to awards,
which was broadly in line with our expectations. However, both individual and enterprise arrangements
surprised us to the upside, contributing 0.64ppts and 0.65ppts to the quarterly increase in wages
respectively.
National minimum wage and award rates, regulated by the Fair Work Ombudsman,
Enterprise agreements, or
Individual arrangements.

### Page 4

Compared to the same quarter in previous years, each stream's contribution was the strongest in
absolute terms since 2023, largely because the overall lift was bigger than in 2024 and 2025. After
accounting for the size of the increase, enterprise arrangements were responsible for 64% of the lift,
whereas individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share since 2019.
While the larger award wage decision pushed up the overall increase, it did not flow through evenly.

Many enterprise agreements are directly linked to award outcomes, either through explicit reference to
award rates or because the award decision sets a benchmark in bargaining, which helps explain why
this stream was more responsive. By contrast, wages set under individual arrangements can be more
discretionary and depend more on employers' circumstances, so they were less responsive than in
previous years. That may reflect a softer labour market, where weaker demand for workers reduces the

pressure on employers to lift pay.
Wages by Sector
By sector, private wages (excl. bonuses) rose 1.0% in Q3, pulling up the annual pace from 3.1%yr to
3.4%yr. This marks the strongest pace of wages growth in the private sector since 2023, at the time of
the Fair Work Commission’s decision to raise award wages by a much larger 5.75%.
Private sector pay is set through a mix of arrangements, including a significant share of individual
arrangements (as discussed above). Today's result suggests this year's award wage decision may

have had some flow-on effect to the broader private sector, though, as noted, the pass-through was
uneven and appears to have been stronger for enterprise arrangements than for individual
arrangements.
There was also an element of both breadth and scale when it comes to private sector wage gains. Half
of all private sector jobs recorded a wage increase in Q3, the largest share since this series began in
2016; and of those jobs that recorded an increase, average hourly wages rose 4.5%, the strongest

since Q3 2023 (5.8%).
Public wages continue to outpace the private sector, this time with a 1.3% lift in Q3, bringing the annual
growth pace to 3.9%yr, the strongest since Q4 2025. As expected, the bulk of the gain was driven by
pay rises under enterprise agreements at the state government level, particularly for front-line workers.
Additionally, given scheduled rises under existing APS-wide enterprise agreements for Commonwealth
employees had already been delivered in previous quarters, the Commonwealth level contribution to

public sector wages growth during Q3 was modest.
Relative to the private sector, the driver of public sector wage gains looks to be more about scale than
breadth – across the 38% of public sector jobs recording a wage increase during the quarter the
average hourly wage change was 4.0%, slightly under the record high of a 4.3% recorded back in Q4
2023.

### Page 5

Summary
The Q3 result shows that the larger minimum wage and award rate decision boosted wages but the
detail suggests the lift was driven more by workers with stronger bargaining power. Awards and
enterprise arrangements, where pay outcomes are more directly tied to the Fair Work Commission’s
decision or to collective bargaining, whereas individual arrangements, which are more exposed to
labour market conditions, contributed the smallest share since 2019.

That is consistent with a softer labour market – the unemployment rate has risen ½ppt since the start of
the year, and we are expecting it to peak at 5.2% mid next year. The NAB business survey points the
same way, with the increase in labour costs reported by private sector firms following the award
decision quickly normalising. This suggests the Q3 result is better read as a temporary step up in the
level of wages from the award wage decision than as evidence of building underlying wage pressure,

though we will be watching whether the strength in enterprise bargaining persists.

级资产负债表 ” 。

亿美元股票，数⽇后⼜定价发⾏ 250 亿美元⾼评级公司债券。

债务置于表外，使公司能够在不牺牲评级的情况下加杠杆，这对处在资本开⽀竞赛中的⼤型科
技企业尤为重要。
分销更⼴，买家更多
SEC 规则下的发⾏⽅式，让这笔债券可以触达更⼴的机构群体。
Manulife Investment Management 固收⾼级投资组合经理 Jeff Given 表⽰，这带来了⼴泛分销与
灵活性，意味着债券可能 “ 落⼊更多投资者⼿中 ” 。对于⼀笔规模超过 300 亿美元的发⾏⽽⾔，投
资者基础的宽度直接关系到定价与⼆级市场表现。
从 6 ⽉的 IPO 、随后的⾼评级债券，到如今的芯⽚租赁融资， SpaceX 在数⽉内搭建起⼀套多层次
的资本结构。周四的定价结果，将检验市场对 AI 相关债务的胃⼝还能承接多⼤的供给。

Okusanya (REITs), Peter Abramowitz (office REITs), Mark DeVries (consumer and 
specialty finance), Nicole DeBlase (MI/EE), Corinne Blanchard (clean tech), 
Richa Harnain (transports), and Scott Deuschle (aerospace and defense).  
With the 2026 US midterm elections approaching, we look at key investor 
debates surrounding the event and legislative priorities of the next Congress. 
Despite the uncertainty that midterms can bring, the market appears fairly 
confident on expectations for the overall results. While a surprise Republican-
majority victory in both chambers is still possible, consensus is converging 
around current polling indicating Democrats are likely to flip the seats needed for 
a House majority and may win a Senate majority. Historically, an incumbent 
president has lost seats in 20 of the last 22 midterms. Investor expectations are 
in line with this. Polymarket, for example, is currently above 90% for Democrats 
gaining control of the House and above 60% for Democrats gaining control of the 
Senate.  
With this set of expectations, key investor debates instead focus on the 
legislative agenda for hot-ticket policy issues. Namely, these hinge on AI and 
data centers, affordability (affecting healthcare, housing, and education loans), 
and economic and fiscal policy. Debates also surround possible influence of the 
new Congress on the war in Iran and, by extension, energy prices. In this report, 
we cut through the noise on what the next Congress could reasonably advance 
from these agendas, what the impact would be on affected sectors, and how to 
address market uncertainty – and perceived certainty – into the elections. 
Historically, midterm elections have proven to be a check on administration 
policies. The number of seats lost in the midterms by the incumbent party is tied 
to the president’s approval rating going into midterms, which in turn tends to 
align with consumer confidence. President Trump’s ~38% approval rating, at a 
record low for his tenure, would suggest a little over a 45-seat loss for the 
Republican Party (in the House and Senate combined). That said, polling in the 
Senate races that will determine control of the chamber largely remains within 
the margin of error, and in 2024, Trump won in at least five of the seven core toss-
up states. Thus, the Senate remains a much closer call than the House race.

Whatever the outcome of the midterms, the legislative agenda will 
still face constraints from the White House. It is all but impossible for either party 
to gain a veto-proof majority in either chamber, minimizing the likelihood of a 
large shift in fiscal or regulatory policy. The most immediately obvious impact of 
Democrats taking the Senate would be the ability to slow or block executive 
branch appointees, including at the Federal Reserve Board of Governors or the 
Supreme Court, should any members step down before their terms end.

### Page 3

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 3

Without garnering a two-thirds majority in both chambers, Democrats lack a clear 
path to unwind the 2025 tax cuts or enact AI-related regulatory legislation – at 
least, not without President Trump’s explicit approval. With respect to the near-
term fiscal policy outlook, the most important period for investors may be the 
“lame duck” session, the period after the November 3 election and before the new 
Congress convenes on January 3. Recall that current federal government funding 
expires on December 11, and the Trump administration is asking for a nearly 60% 
increase in defense spending, the largest since the Korean War. In addition, the 
debt limit will become binding again around the middle of next year. In short, the 
actions during the lame duck session could determine whether the new Congress 
is seated amid a government shutdown, a contentious battle over the federal 
budget, and the debt limit looming in the background.  
In terms of the market reaction to midterm elections, the risk-reward profile has 
typically been favorable, with 21 of the last 23 midterm years seeing positive 
returns in the fourth quarter, with an average gain of 7% in the S&P. The gains are 
notable even after adjusting for typical positive year-end seasonality.

Figure 3: S&P Returns and Midterms

Source: Bloomberg Finance LP

It would not be unusual for the market to go sideways as investors hedge 
potential volatility and uncertainty, as we have seen previously ahead of other 
well-telegraphed pivotal events. This has historically been followed by a rally as 
the results became clear and uncertainty dissipated, regardless of the result 
itself. In the current context, if election polls and prediction market probabilities 
continue being lopsided, the market may not treat the election as an uncertain 
risk event going in. Uncertainty could still ramp up after, and the typical midterm 
election rally may be pushed out. It is notable that the one prominent exception 
to the midterm rallies was in 2018, when the market sold off sharply after the 
elections as uncertainty spiked around trade war 1.0 as well as Fed policy.

### Page 4

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 4

We note that the midterm elections also coincide with the third quarter earnings 
season, during which we expect companies to deliver growth of 34%. For next 
year, we expect growth to be a still-robust 17% with risks to the upside. 
Eventually, earnings tend to be the anchor for equities and have been for several 
years despite numerous domestic political as well as geopolitical shocks. 
Across sectors, most have rallied around past midterms in keeping with the 
market, but there isn’t a consistent pattern of out- or underperformance. This 
suggests that for the initial conditions for each sector, specific policies and 
positioning played a bigger role. For that reason, we turn to a closer analysis of 
key legislative and policy debates impacting exposed sectors.

### Page 5

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 5

AI and Data Centers  
AI and data centers have become a campaign talking point for candidates from 
both the Democratic and Republican parties. This has spurred investor questions 
on whether to expect a national regulatory framework constraining data center 
buildouts. Some of the headlines surrounding this debate overestimate what 
Congress can accomplish through national legislation compared with what is 
possible at a state level, particularly during a presidential administration 
supportive of AI and data center acceleration. Still, the industry is responding to 
a political backdrop that is increasingly scrutinizing AI and data centers.  
The current administration has prioritized advancing AI and digital infrastructure 
development. The White House’s AI Action Plan, released July 2025, included a 
set of executive orders supporting the rapid buildout of AI data centers by 
reducing certain federal regulatory and permitting burdens. The administration 
did not preempt state laws on data center infrastructure development and 
federal permitting orders generally do not override state zoning and utility rules. 
In March 2026, the White House announced the Ratepayer Protection Pledge, 
under which large data center operators would voluntarily commit to covering 
the cost to upgrade generation, transmission, substations, or other necessary 
infrastructure to service these developments. The industry’s response to these 
proposals has been largely constructive. The Data Center Coalition (DCC), an 
industry group representing major hyperscalers and data center owners and 
operators came out in support of the initial version of the Pledge, while arguing 
that large-load customers across all industries also should cover the costs to 
upgrade energy infrastructure. According to the DCC, “the data center 
industry remains committed to being responsible partners where we operate, 
which includes paying our full energy costs and investing in local jobs and 
workforce development.” 
Congress voted in September 2026 to codify the principles present in the Pledge 
via the Ratepayer Protection Act. The bill, which only required states to “consider” 
these standards, passed overwhelmingly in the House (417-3), but failed to reach 
60 votes in the Senate (57-43). Critics noted the bill stopped short of imposing 
mandatory nationwide rules. 
Looking forward, we expect a Congress that is more focused on consumer 
protection might look to codify a more stringent version of these rules, requiring 
large-load customers to bear the full incremental costs associated with 
generation, transmission, and distribution upgrades. However, even if such a 
framework were passed into law, we believe the overall economic impact to large 
data center operators would be relatively modest, for two reasons. 
For one, the White House would maintain a veto over bills that include language 
it views as overly burdensome towards AI infrastructure development. Two, data 
center operators generally underwrite projects only when they have a high 
degree of confidence in securing power and, where necessary, may already be 
embedding the cost of upgrading energy infrastructure into their investment 
plans. We expect developers to be capable of passing on sustained increases in 
power infrastructure costs to their customers, which, for many 100 MW+ 
developments, are the major investment-grade hyperscalers.

### Page 6

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 6

Though data center development is receiving increasing national attention, it is 
still largely a local issue, and the most consequential elections will be at the state 
and local levels. We have seen a number of state-driven actions on data centers 
in recent months, including: 
• In July, New York State’s governor issued an executive order to establish 
a temporary moratorium on data center development while the state 
develops a standardized framework of rules for key issues (including 
responsibilities for power and energy infrastructure upgrades; additional 
power generation; clean energy; water use, emission, and waste 
management; and state/local financial incentives). The New York 
legislature also passed a one-year moratorium on Department of 
Environmental Conservation permits and approvals for 20 MW+ data 
center developments. 
• In August, Texas’ governor directed the state’s public utility commission 
to conduct a comprehensive audit of every data center advancing 
through the ERCOT (Texas’s grid operator) interconnection process. The 
audit will cover several notable areas including public financial support, 
electricity demand and supply, water use, community impacts, and 
ownership and control. 
• In September, California’s governor passed a comprehensive regulatory 
package, covering electricity, water, reporting, and environmental 
review. Most of these provisions take effect January 1, 2027, and phase 
in through 2027-2028, but the package does not ban data centers or 
impose a statewide moratorium on development activity. 
• Oregon, Pennsylvania, New Jersey, and other states have also 
established orders to create standards and frameworks governing data 
center development (but without imposing broad moratoriums). 
At the more local level, a large number of moratoriums have been passed on new 
data center development. Data collected by SemiAnalysis, an independent 
research firm, shows that, as of September, more than 300 local data center 
moratoriums have been adopted nationwide across counties, municipalities, 
townships, and other local governments.  
However, these state and local moratoriums appear thus far to be less impactful 
to near- and medium-term development activity than headline coverage might 
otherwise suggest. According to SemiAnalysis, only about 2.3 GW of the 38 GW 
of data center capacity planned or under construction for 2027 has faced delays 
as a result of the New York state executive order (0.8 GW) and/or local 
moratoriums (1.5 GW). 
We believe the primary reasons that moratoriums are, in reality, less likely to 
impact near-term development activity are (1) that these moratoriums largely 
impact new projects and applications (rather than existing developments in 
progress), (2) there may be flexibility to relocate or rezone early-stage projects in 
response to moratoriums, and (3) data center developers are increasingly 
partnering with local communities to gain their support via investments and other 
programs (as we wrote about recently). Potential regulatory risk has not yet 
slowed capex spending patterns, with capex spend still expected to climb from

### Page 7

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 7

$0.5 trillion in 2025 to $1.3 trillion by 2027; data centers typically represent ~20-
30% of the total bill of materials for new AI capacity.  
Although the practical impact on near- and medium-term development pipelines 
appears less pronounced than headlines might suggest, all this is not to say that 
data centers will not face increasing local opposition following the midterms. 
Indeed, in our conversations with industry participants, there is an 
acknowledgment that greater public scrutiny of data centers is not going away. 
Additional moratoriums, zoning restrictions, permitting requirements, or utility-
related conditions could affect the pace, cost, and overall feasibility of future 
projects.

### Page 8

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 8

Affordability Agenda: Healthcare, Housing, and 
Education Loans  
The affordability agenda has become a centerpiece of many Democratic as well 
as Republican campaign platforms. This platform is impacting investor 
debates across healthcare, housing, and student loans. In healthcare, the push 
among some Democrats for expanded health coverage under the Medicare for All 
banner is placing pressure on managed care stocks, but meanwhile, companies 
with exposure to Medicaid and the Affordable Care Act (ACA) could see a 
supportive political backdrop. On housing, investor debates on the impacts of the 
affordability agenda have continued since passage of legislation in July 2026 that 
caps the number of single-family homes for-profit institutional investors can 
purchase. Affordability of higher education is likewise part of the conversation, 
leading investors to debate which levers Congress could pull that would affect 
student loan companies.  
Healthcare 
In healthcare, we see the upcoming midterm elections as a sentiment event for 
managed care stocks, not a policy event. With the presidential veto in place 
through 2028, we do not expect the government to enact direct healthcare 
legislation during the next Congress. Forecasts place the probability of a change 
in House control at roughly 79%, with both chambers changing in about 44% of 
simulations. Although investor skittishness around Medicare for All (M4A) rhetoric 
is high, we see the likelihood of M4A replacing private coverage as a near-zero 
probability in this timeframe.  
M4A has no realistic legislative path before 2029, but the rhetoric weighs on 
managed care organization (MCO) stock multiples by raising perceived long-term 
risk rather than affecting near-term earnings. Polling from Kaiser Family 
Foundation, a health policy research organization, shows support for M4A at a 
decade-plus high, and the 2018–2019 cycle suggests that increased campaign 
attention can pressure MCO multiples even without a direct policy impact. We 
expect similar sentiment-driven pressure through year-end and potentially into 
the 2028 primary cycle but would not view it as a change in near-term 
fundamentals.  
For Medicare Advantage, we see a headline risk over earnings reality. The near-
term earnings impact for affected companies should be limited, because the US 
Centers for Medicare & Medicaid Services (CMS) has finalized a 2.48% Medicare 
Advantage (MA) rate increase for 2027, versus 0.09% in the advance notice. A 
change in House control would instead increase focus on 2028 payment policy, 
risk adjustment, and prior authorization. Coding reform already has bipartisan 
support: sponsors estimate that the Cassidy-Merkley No UPCODE Act could 
generate up to $275 billion of savings over ten years, while MedPAC estimates 
coding intensity will add roughly $40 billion to 2025 Medicare Advantage costs 
relative to traditional Medicare. In our view, the more immediate consideration is 
potential multiple pressure rather than a measurable change to company 
earnings.  
However, the next Congress could benefit Medicaid funding and ACA policy. 
Medicaid and the ACA appear relatively better positioned, although any benefit

### Page 9

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 9

would likely be reflected first in sentiment and lower perceived policy risk rather 
than near-term estimate revisions. Medicaid community engagement 
requirements take effect in most states by January 1, 2027. Although repeal 
remains unlikely given the presidential veto, a change in House control could shift 
the year-end funding debate toward restoring or preserving federal Medicaid 
support, which could ease state budget pressure and support rate-setting. The 
ACA policy backdrop could also become more supportive, although we see 
limited scope for enhanced premium subsidies to return before 2029 after the 
three-year extension passed by the House stalled.  
Housing 
Turning to the housing sector, the likely midterm outcome of a split government 
would result in more gridlock. Historically, political gridlock in terms of passing 
new regulation has been a tailwind for the real estate sector, reducing regulatory 
risk at the federal level. In the scenario of a split Congress, in which the Democrats 
have a majority in the House but not the Senate, bills with bipartisan support have 
a greater likelihood to become law. Two key areas in which this bipartisan support 
exists are housing affordability and consumer protection. 
Among real estate investment trusts (REITs), we see Residential as among the 
most impacted subsectors, particularly as it relates to the affordability debate. 
Regardless of the election outcome, housing affordability has become front and 
center on the political agenda across both parties. Federal legislation is more 
likely to target supply-side solutions, as legislation restricting rent increases is 
typically handled at the local and state level. We think Multifamily would be the 
most likely subsector impacted, as a push to expand rental housing supply could 
lead to eased zoning restrictions, particularly in supply-constrained coastal 
markets that have historically benefited from higher barriers to entry. That said, 
this would likely take years to materialize. With a Democratic majority, Congress 
could voice opposition to the influence of large institutional landlords, but we see 
this strictly as a sentiment risk to stocks.  
Single Family For Rent (SFR) is less exposed to near-term legislative shocks, as 
the sector was a focus of the 21st Century ROAD to Housing Act passed in July 
2026 over the summer. The measure impacts institutional owners’ ability to buy 
homes off the MLS but contains specific carve-out provisions that will allow them 
to keep developing build-to-rent (BTR) product and buy excess inventory from 
homebuilders, the sector's preferred acquisition channel in recent years. The 
ROAD to Housing Act also contained provisions that could theoretically ease the 
production of manufactured homes, the main business segment for 
Manufactured Housing (MH) REITs. The first-order impact is likely more flexibility 
in home designs that could lead to further densification of MH communities, 
although we note emerging investor debate about the long-term impacts of 
increased MH production on competitive supply for a property type that has 
historically been supply constrained, mainly due to NIMBY-ism.  
Parallel to the conversation on affordability, it is clear that, regardless of which 
political party may be in power following the midterms, a major focus remains 
addressing the US fiscal deficit and its continued growth. We continue to pay 
attention to potential budget cuts or sequestration to slow government spending 
and reduce the deficit, which could have an impact on real estate. Key areas to 
watch include cuts to healthcare spending (which would be a negative for skilled

### Page 10

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 10

nursing focused REITs, given Medicare is a major revenue source for their tenant 
base) and cuts that would impact Office REITs with large government exposure.  
We also worry that an elevated federal deficit and the elevated Treasury 
borrowing needs today create a risk that interest rates will remain higher for 
longer. This puts pressure on real estate valuations and cost of financing for REITs 
to drive external growth via acquisitions and development. A split Congress is 
likely to result in more difficult budget negotiations and increased oversight, but 
it remains unclear what the ultimate outcome would be to address the budget 
deficit issue. 
With real estate being more dependent on local market dynamics, we believe the 
more likely regulatory impact would come from local regulation. This could show 
up on the ballots during the midterms, with increased possibility of rent control 
legislation targeted at residential real estate as well as self-storage, as cities and 
municipalities focus on affordability issues. There is also growing legislative 
pushback at the local level against development of real assets such as data 
centers given the large power requirements, as discussed in detail elsewhere in 
this report.  
Education Lending 
Midterm campaign platforms on education affordability have a bearing on the 
consumer and specialty finance sector. Much of the policy exposure in this sector 
is through the executive branch bodies, not Congress, however. This includes, for 
example, regulatory authorities such as the FDIC (Federal Deposit Insurance 
Corporation), the CFPB (Consumer Financial Protection Bureau), or the FHFA 
(Federal Housing Finance Agency, which regulates Fannie Mae and Freddie 
Mac). That said, historical data shows that student loan company stocks can still 
directionally align with midterm party outcome and expectations in Congress, 
although the sample size is small.

### Page 11

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 11

Mixed Impacts of Economic and Fiscal Policy  
Democratic and Republican candidates have aimed to distinguish themselves 
from their opponents along a range of economic and fiscal policy matters, 
including tariffs, tax, labor, and the budget. In reality, the outcome of the 
midterms, regardless of which party prevails, is likely to present a mixture of relief 
and restrictions affecting businesses. In some cases, investor fears over sweeping 
economic policy changes are overblown for topics over which Congress 
realistically has limited influence, presenting more headline risk than a risk to 
fundamentals. In considering these, we look to industrials that are particularly 
sensitive to key investor debates, spanning multi-industry/electrical equipment, 
clean tech and alternative energy, transport, and defense.  
For the multi-industry and electrical equipment (MI/EE) sector, historical midterm 
election cycles suggest a generally constructive backdrop heading into election 
day, with positive returns in six of the past seven elections. On average, the MI/EE 
group has gained ~5% during the three months preceding historical midterm 
elections and ~8% during the final month prior. Relative outperformance versus 
the S&P 500 has been less consistent; we observed outperformance in only four 
of seven cycles, though the group still generated average excess returns of 
~250bps over the preceding three months and ~270bps over the preceding 
month.  
Overall, the data supports a favorable absolute return backdrop ahead of 
midterms, but significant stock-level dispersion suggests company-specific 
fundamentals and factor leadership have historically mattered more than the 
election cycle alone. We mapped out election results scenarios across key policy 
pillars for our coverage (tax policy and capital investment; trade, tariffs, and 
supply chains; energy, grid, and power infrastructure; AI and data center 
regulation; and the regulatory environment). We focus on two key scenarios: one 
in which Republicans maintain control of both chambers, and a divided 
government that sees the Democrats controlling one or both chambers of the 
legislative branch.  
Under a divided government, we expect stock-level dispersion would likely 
narrow, with globally exposed companies receiving some trade policy relief and 
HVAC/automation companies benefiting from a potentially more supportive 
regulatory backdrop. However, should Republicans maintain control of Congress, 
our scenario analysis suggests this would be favorable for electrical equipment 
and power infrastructure manufacturers, due to a combination of pro-investment 
tax policy, faster infrastructure deployment, and a lighter AI regulatory 
framework. That said, globally exposed companies could face incremental tariff 
and retaliation risks.  
Looking to the clean tech and alternative energy sector, the midterm elections 
could represent a positive tailwind. The next Congress might be positioned to 
pursue targeted extensions or modifications to certain clean-energy tax credits. 
The introduction of H.R. 8477, which proposes to restore several clean energy tax 
credits rolled back in late 2025, seems to signal that some, albeit a limited 
number, of Republicans could be supportive of this initiative. If the Democrats 
gain a majority in the House but not the Senate in the midterms, it is plausible that 
Democrats could find sufficient Republican support to advance

### Page 12

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 12

legislation extending clean-energy credits, though they would still face 
significant legislative hurdles. A midterm outcome in which Republicans retain 
control of both chambers is likely to maintain the status quo for the remaining 
existing tax credits, especially for those tied to domestic manufacturing. While 
those could potentially see further extension, we think this is a limited outcome.    
While we see a low probability of a major change in, or support for, the residential 
tax credits that expired at the end of 2025 (25D), we envision a scenario in which 
indirect residential tax credits (48E) could be extended, which would help boost 
part of the demand. Softness in demand is expected to remain going into 2027, 
still impacted by the phaseout of the 25D tax credits, the eventual roll-off of safe-
harbor benefits, and a more constrained financing environment.  
From a manufacturing tax credit standpoint, there could be additional tailwinds 
if we were to see any manufacturing tax credit extension or positive modification 
(although lower probability, in our view). This would also likely improve utility-
scale project economics if broader project-related credits are preserved, though 
upcoming elections could cause a slowdown in new booking activity until 
legislative uncertainty clears.  
For Transports, the midterms would be most relevant for the trucking supply 
thesis. Truckload stocks could have a modest negative reaction to a Democratic 
majority if investors expect some rollback of the Trump administration’s 
constraints on drivers, which has tightened supply. We would separate 
perception from fundamentals, however. The election of a Democratic Congress 
would not automatically by itself change leadership at the Department of 
Transportation (DOT) and the Federal Motor Carrier Safety Administration 
(FMCSA), the executive branch entities driving much of the current push for 
restrictions on drivers.  
One possible impact of the midterms for Transports is if a change in 
Congressional control contributed to a faster resolution of the war in Iran. Any 
resulting lower energy prices would be a positive for Transports. We view this as 
a lower-probability outcome, however, given the constraints on a divided 
government.  
With clashes over the budget likely looming, aerospace and defense could get 
caught in the crosshairs. While defense spending has historically enjoyed 
bipartisan support, it has grown politicized following the war in Iran, making it 
more difficult for a split Congress to pass such a budget. The defense budget has 
experienced significant growth over the last few years, rising from $900 billion in 
FY2024 to $1 trillion in FY2026. The Trump administration would like to see this 
trend accelerate: in April 2026, the president issued a budget request that called 
for a $1.5 trillion budget for the Department of Defense in FY2027. With a 
continuing resolution likely into year-end, the outcome of the midterm elections 
will determine whether the next Congress can pass such a budget. If Republicans 
hold the House and Senate, we think there is a reasonable prospect of a budget 
of $1.2 trillion or more passing into law. Defense investors would see that as a 
substantial tailwind for beaten-up defense stocks. On the other hand, this would 
be unlikely in a split Congress, in our view. Given all this, we see the outcome of 
the upcoming midterm elections as key to determining how US defense stocks 
will perform into year end and beyond.

### Page 13

23 October 2026 
The Theme Screen

Deutsche Bank Securities Inc. Page 13

Taking Stock of the Known Unknowns 
Although Republicans could maintain control of both chambers, consensus is 
converging around polls pointing to a Democratic majority in the House and 
possibly the Senate. The perceived certainty has allowed the investor debate 
related to midterms to focus not only on who will win but how they will govern. In 
some cases, headlines are misrepresenting what a Congress constrained by the 
executive will be able to accomplish. In other areas, legislation that is expected 
to be passed will need to be taken together with offsetting factors. Whatever the 
outcome, the political backdrop will continue to influence the market and 
investor debates in the year ahead.

### Page 14

Deutsche Bank Research Institute 
Swing State: Navigating Midterm Volatility – October 23, 2026

Cảng HKQT Long Thành giai đoạn 1 dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 
12/2026 và bắt đầu tiếp nhận các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
vào ngày 28/03/2027, bổ sung công suất 25 triệu khách/năm cho ACV. Tuy nhiên,

trong khi chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng dự kiến gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028.

LẬP đối với ACV với giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng 
giá 4.5% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 18/11/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

ACV hiện đang quản lý, đầu tư và khai thác 
hệ thống 21 Cảng hàng không trong cả nước, 
bao gồm 10 Cảng hàng không quốc tế và 11 
Cảng hàng không nội địa. Ngành nghề kinh 
doanh chính của ACV là hoạt động cung cấp 
dịch vụ hỗ trợ trực tiếp cho vận tải hàng 
không, bao gồm dịch vụ hàng không, dịch vụ 
phi hàng không và bán hàng. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Tăng trưởng bền vững đến từ cả nhóm khách nội địa và quốc tế, tốc độ tăng trung bình dự kiến đạt 
5.5% và 7%/năm giai đoạn 2026-2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Long Thành bổ sung 25 triệu khách/năm từ 2027, song khấu hao tăng mạnh khiến lợi nhuận chạm 
đáy năm 2028 trước khi phục hồi từ 2029. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Quá trình lên HOSE của ACV tiến triển thuận lợi nhờ các tài sản nhà nước đang dần được tách bạch, 
tạo thuận lợi cho việc hoàn tất cổ phần hóa tài sản khu bay cho phần vốn nhà nước. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây

HOẠT ĐỘNG KINH DOANH 
KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu 
hao và biến động tỷ giá

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty 
mẹ đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao 
tăng từ các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược 
với khoản lãi tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty 
mẹ lần lượt đạt 19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Biểu đồ 6. Các quốc gia có lượng khách đến Việt Nam lớn nhất trong 10M2026 (khách) 
Trung Quốc và Hàn Quốc tiếp tục là hai thị 
trường khách quốc tế lớn nhất đến Việt Nam 
trong 10M2026 nhưng tăng trưởng hạn chế 
(+1.4% và -6.3% yoy), chủ yếu chịu tác động 
từ giá nhiên liệu tăng cao, khiến các hãng 
hàng không cắt giảm tần suất bay và du 
khách có xu hướng ưu tiên các điểm đến gần. 
Ngược lại, khách châu Âu tăng mạnh (+51.5% 
yoy), chủ yếu nhờ (1) thị trường Nga phục hồi 
(+150% yoy) sau khi các đường bay thẳng và 
charter được khôi phục, mở rộng và (2) hưởng 
lợi từ chính sách miễn thị thực bổ sung cho 12 
quốc gia châu Âu từ tháng 8/2025 và việc tăng 
cường kết nối hàng không trong năm 2026.

Nguồn: GSO

Triển vọng tăng trưởng hành khách 
tích cực, song ACV dự kiến tăng 
chậm hơn toàn ngành 
 Theo dự báo của Bộ Xây dựng, lượng hành khách thông qua các cảng hàng 
không Việt Nam dự kiến tăng trưởng bình quân 9.7%/năm giai đoạn 2026–
2030, đạt 191.2 triệu lượt vào năm 2030. Về dài hạn, Airbus dự báo lưu lượng 
hành khách hàng không Việt Nam tăng trưởng 7.7%/năm trong 20 năm tới, gần 
gấp khoảng 2 lần mức trung bình toàn cầu (3.9%/năm). Các động lực chính bao 
gồm triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế, nhu cầu du lịch gia tăng, chính sách miễn 
thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng đường bay quốc tế, cùng kế hoạch bổ 
sung đội bay của các hãng hàng không.

### Page 5

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế và nội địa của ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân lần lượt 7% và 5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn mức tăng trưởng chung của 
toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc được chuyển ra khỏi hệ thống ACV từ đầu 
2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia 
Bình khi sân bay này đi vào khai thác. Chúng tôi đánh giá mức độ dịch chuyển 
trước năm 2030 còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ xây dựng sân bay và hạ 
tầng kết nối. Bên cạnh đó, đề xuất chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài 
sang Gia Bình hiện chưa được phê duyệt và chưa được phản ánh trong dự 
phóng. Nếu được thông qua, đề xuất này có thể ảnh hưởng đáng kể đến lợi 
nhuận của ACV, do lợi nhuận trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao gấp khoảng 4–5 lần 
khách nội địa, đặc biệt trong giai đoạn sau năm 2030.

Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp  Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp

Cảng HKQT Long Thành mở ra dư 
địa tăng trưởng dài hạn, song tạo 
áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong 
ngắn và trung hạn 
 Cảng HKQT Long Thành dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 12/2026, chủ 
yếu phục vụ các chuyến bay nội địa và một số chuyến quốc tế. Hoạt động 
chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài và vận tải hàng hóa từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
dự kiến bắt đầu từ ngày 28/03/2027. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh giá tốc độ dịch 
chuyển có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện hạ tầng 
giao thông kết nối với trung tâm TP.HCM, đặc biệt là dự án mở rộng cao tốc 
TP.HCM – Long Thành – Dầu Giây và các dự án kết nối đường sắt bao gồm Thủ 
Thiêm – Long Thành.

Dự án cảng HKQT Long Thành kỳ vọng sẽ mở ra dư địa tăng trưởng dài hạn cho 
ACV, dù gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu khai thác. Việc đưa 
Long Thành vào vận hành sẽ giúp ACV mở rộng công suất, giảm áp lực quá tải 
tại Tân Sơn Nhất, đồng thời tạo điều kiện thu hút thêm các đường bay quốc tế, 
đặc biệt là đường bay dài và hoạt động vận tải hàng hóa. Tuy nhiên, trong ngắn 
và trung hạn, chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng trong khi sản lượng hành 
khách cần thời gian để đạt công suất tối ưu dự kiến sẽ gây áp lực lên biên lợi 
nhuận của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028. Trong mô hình dự phóng, chúng tôi ghi 
nhận khấu hao Long Thành theo tiến độ nghiệm thu, kết chuyển và đưa vào sử 
dụng từng hạng mục tài sản cố định, thay vì toàn bộ dự án ngay khi bắt đầu vận 
hành.

MR nhà ga T1 Cảng HKQT Đà Nẵng 9,936 6 8 2027-2029 
2025 đã hoạt động vượt 25% công suất thiết kế. Dự án mở rộng 
nâng tổng công suất nhà ga T1 lên 10 triệu khách quốc nội và 4 
triệu khách quốc tế/năm. Dự kiến khởi công tháng 8/2027, hoàn 
thành phần nhà ga mở rộng vào đầu 2029, cải tạo nhà ga hiện 
hữu dự kiến hoàn thành tháng 8/2029. 
Nhà ga T2 Cát Bi - Hải Phòng 2,690 2 5 2024-2027 
Khởi công từ tháng 8/2025, dự kiến hoàn thành tháng 5/2027. 
Có phương án dự trù mở rộng lên 10 triệu khách/năm khi có 
nhu cầu. 
Nhà ga T2 - Đồng Hới 1,843 0.5 3 2024-2026 Bao gồm nhà ga hành khách và công trình phụ trợ trên diện tích 1.2 ha, khởi công tháng 4/2025, dự kiến vận hành từ 4Q2026. 
Mở rộng CHK Cà Mau 2,400 0.3 0.2 2024-2026 
Khởi công vào tháng 4/2025. Công suất có thể mở rộng lên 1 
triệu khách khi có nhu cầu. Hiện đang triển khai khẩn trương 
các hạng mục trọng yếu, kế hoạch khai thác lại từ cuối tháng 
11/2026.
